# CDH-0019: remove redundant sublines

Michou directly instructed this separate cycle at 2026-10-26T17:50:00-07:00, recorded on the README’s Asked-and-answered line. It starts from the completed precision commit 5a03da1. **PASS standalone:** the final gate passes 486 tests with 0 blocking lint; both engines preserve every board-wide default and all-state minimum against CDH-0032, and every measured applicable text pair is at least 4.5:1. The report lists every decrease greater than 0.3 by name and ground. Native review remains Cowork’s.

## Delivered cuts

All eleven boards and the station were inspected. Thirty-seven copy cuts affect nine surface sources. Boards 4 (Blocking), 6 (Disconnected) and 7 (Keys) have no qualifying removals: their explanatory lines carry ledger context, recovery or interpretation rules, reserved-key meanings and bindings. Whole helper elements are removed only when their entire text qualifies; mixed lines retain their existing factual, rule or key fragments. The two demo-note render branches omit the whole helper caption when its note becomes empty, including the redundant idle/stale labels. No new visible wording, CSS, component arrangement or operating behavior is introduced. Released line space accounts for the layout movement.

Board 1’s noise rule, every key legend including duplicates, every snapshot/as-of boundary, the quiet threshold and elapsed-time origin remain. Actor biographies and Voice examples remain as the specimens being reviewed, rather than helper copy. Corner micro-labels and accessible names remain unchanged.

## Preservation checks

The twelve-source comparison against 5a03da1 passes: every CSS block, ARIA attribute, key span, visually hidden span, corner micro-label and SVG symbol is byte-identical. All scripts are byte-identical except Tray’s three helper-note strings. All default render values remain identical except those three notes; ledger and state arrays are unchanged. The specimen-only check passes 123 static tests and twelve projections with no template holes or dangling note separators. The full precision gate checks the final source geometry after the released space.

## Contrast and gate

The final make check passes: 486 tests, 10 agent tests deselected, ruff clean; kgme lint reports 236 notes, 0 blocking, 134 advisory and 6 cosmetic. It was rerun after the last Tray-caption removal; no code or source layout changes followed. Final document lint is checked separately. The baseline is the hash-verified precision commit: Safari captures are independently copied, Chromium evidence is referenced by hash with default visuals copied for diffs, and the twelve source hashes are checked before reuse. CDH-0031 evidence is preserved. The corrected opaque-core method and unchanged-range swatch fallback are reused, including disclosure, scroll, phone and reduced-motion states. The CDH-0032 floor comparison uses the same-engine, same-method baseline retained in the precision report. JetBrains Mono remains uncertified.

Safari’s first station attempt timed out before any capture because its document was hidden. A bounded diagnostic verified all assets loaded, zero script errors and healthy WebGL, while rAF never initialized the replay. After bringing Safari forward through native UI and selecting its WebDriver window, the same diagnostic observed a visible document and 37 events within 2 seconds. The final sweep asserts visibility before each station variant and records zero changed original ranges. No timer-driven replacement renderer or design-source patch was used. The lessons file records this visibility precondition within its 40-line limit.

### Shared sampler coverage correction

The final inventory comparison found eleven visibly painted station bubble identities omitted by the inherited pointer hit-test because their elements use pointer-events:none. This affected the earlier precision evidence as well as the first declutter sweep. The correction is a measurement change; every design source remains frozen. Safari recovers opaque glyph cores from the existing all-text foreground/white/background masks, using original direct-node ranges and excluding sibling text. Chromium’s stored swatches did not paint the omitted nodes, so a fresh supplement measures all three versions in 24 deck/pane, desktop/phone and normal/reduced-motion cases. Bubble swatches use every device pixel (0.5 CSS px at DPR 2), with unchanged original ranges.

All eleven bubble identities pass in CDH-0032, precision and declutter, with the same 4.5925801817 minimum and zero drops greater than 0.3. Chromium’s corrected station default minimum is therefore 4.5925801817 in all three versions, replacing the incomplete 4.9131326053 value; its all-state minimum already had the corrected value. The corrected unique totals are 204 / 204 / 203, and default-deck counts are 73 in all three versions. Safari’s all-state station minimum remains 4.5299063005; its precision-to-declutter default observations are 1,350 → 1,358 and unique identities 205 → 204. The tables above include these unions without double-counting other text. The precision report carries this explicit addendum, and its original captures remain preserved.

All twelve Safari coreless fragments, including two partial arbiter labels in phone pane defaults and ten scroll fragments, have the exact text identity fully measured in another captured state. Four corner micro-labels are behind the opaque HUD/footer: all 24 nonhidden observations have zero white-versus-background pixel differences, so they are unpainted rather than silently passed text. Hidden and disabled inventories remain explicit. No retained visible identity is unresolved.

## Decisions and boundary

Every individual cut is scored in the table and on the README’s open list. Scores range from .1 to .4, below the .5 approval-screen threshold. Preserving the component reference’s numeric specifications, authored actor/Voice specimens, one visible elapsed-origin definition, every key legend and all accessibility text scores 2 × .2 = .4: these are required content, not removable helper repetition. The brief instruction itself is direct authority and does not change the 10-of-10 recommendation tally. No dependency, feature, control/ or kgme/ change, merge, push, publication or native-review mark is made.

Main was recorded as 30fba471279d397b5411f48285c97acd7d65700e at this resume. It advanced independently through 9d788736e9861b8f8c30335ea0d30dbaff1488b2 to a0f3889ed2216db7854c9bbddecd2c0ca94454e3 during this cycle. The final check confirms that value; this work modified no main file or commit. Cowork’s native pass covers this commit and CDH-0031.
